<script>
dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];

function makeTitle(slug) {
    var words = slug.split('-');

    for(var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
      var word = words[i];
      words[i] = word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1);
    }

    return words.join(' ');
}

function getCookieValue(a) {
    var b = document.cookie.match('(^|;)\\s*' + a + '\\s*=\\s*([^;]+)');
    return b ? b.pop() : '';
}

// Customer Type
if(getCookieValue("_ga")){
    var customerType = 'Guest User';
    var clientId = getCookieValue('_ga').substring(6);
}else{
    var customerType = undefined;
    var clientId = undefined;
}



var contentGroup1 = 'not set',
    contentGroupArray = [],
    breadcrumb = 'Home',
    pageType = undefined;

// Custom Dimension - breadcrumb
if(window.location.pathname == '/') {

} else {
  var current_url = window.location.pathname;
  current_url = current_url.substring(current_url.indexOf('/')+1);
  current_tags = current_url.split('/');

  for (var i = 0; i < current_tags.length; i++) {
    var tag = current_tags[i].split('?')[0];
    breadcrumb += '/' + makeTitle(tag);
  }
}

// content grouping data - 1 - Page Types
if(window.location.pathname == '/') {
  pageType = 'Home';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/products') > -1) {
  pageType = 'Product';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/collections') > -1 && window.location.href.indexOf('/products') == -1) {
  pageType = 'Category';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/cart') > -1 || window.location.href.indexOf('/checkouts') > -1) {
  pageType = 'Checkout';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/blogs') > -1 || window.location.href.indexOf('/pages/about-us') > -1 || window.location.href.indexOf('/pages/careers') > -1){
  pageType = 'Content';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/search?q=') > -1) {
  pageType = 'Search';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/pages/contact-us') > -1) {
  pageType = 'Contact';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/account') > -1) {
  pageType = 'Account';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('/store?') > -1 || window.location.href.indexOf('/store-locator') > -1) {
  pageType = 'Store';
} else {
  pageType = 'Service';
}



if(pageType != 'Product' && pageType !='Category'){
  contentGroup1 = pageType
} else {
  contentGroup1 = 'Assortment';
}

// Content Group 2-5 for collection
if(window.location.href.indexOf('/collections/') > -1) {
  var current_url = window.location.href;
  current_url = current_url.substring(current_url.indexOf('collections')+12);
  current_tags = current_url.split('/');
  for (var i = 0; i < current_tags.length; i++) {
    var tag = current_tags[i].split('?')[0];
    contentGroupArray[i] = makeTitle(tag) == 'Mattresses' ? 'Mattresses & Bases' : makeTitle(tag);
  }
}



if (contentGroupArray.length < 4){
  for (var i = 0; i < (4 - contentGroupArray.length); i++) {
    contentGroupArray.push('not set');
  }
}

// breakpoint custom dimensions
var xsmall = 750,
    small = 769,
    medium = 1024,
    large = 1280,
    xlarge = 1400,
    breakpoint = undefined;
var screenSize = $(window).width();

if (screenSize < xsmall) {
  breakpoint = 'XS';
}
else if (screenSize >= xsmall &&  screenSize <= small) {
  breakpoint = 'XS';
}

else if (screenSize > small &&  screenSize <= medium) {
  breakpoint = 'S';
}
else if (screenSize > medium &&  screenSize <= large) {
  breakpoint = 'M';
}
else if (screenSize > large &&  screenSize <= xlarge) {
  breakpoint = 'L';
}
else  {
  breakpoint = 'XL';
}

// Adblock custom dimensions
// var adBlocker = window['_adblocker'] ? 'false' : 'true';

// queryparms custom dimensions
var queryParams = undefined;
if (window.location.href.indexOf('?') > -1 ){
  queryParams = '?' + window.location.href.split('?')[1];
}

// checkout custom dimension
var funnelType = undefined,
    shippingCity = undefined,
    shippingPostalCode = undefined,
    paymentType = undefined,
    deliveryType = undefined,
    cartCategoryMix = undefined,
    cartCategoryMixArray = [],
    checkoutOption = undefined;

if(window.location.href.indexOf("checkouts") > -1) {
  
    funnelType = 'Guest Funnel';
  

  checkoutOption = '';
  shippingCity = '';
  deliveryType = '';
  shippingPostalCode = '';
}

if(window.location.href.indexOf("thank_you") > -1 && window.location.href.indexOf('checkouts') > -1) {
  cartCategoryMix = '';
  

  cartCategoryMixArray.sort();

  var count = {};
  cartCategoryMixArray.forEach(function(i) { count[i] = (count[i]||0) + 1;});

  var first = true;
  for (i in count) {
    cartCategoryMix += (first ? '' : ', ') + count[i] + ' ' + i ;
    first = false;
  }

  
    paymentType = '';
  
}

var productPageOrderable = undefined,
    recommendationProduct = undefined,
    numberOfStoresWithStock = undefined;

if(window.location.href.indexOf('/products/') > -1){
  
    productPageOrderable = 'true';
  
}

// environment
var environment = undefined;
if(window.location.href.indexOf('localhost') > -1){
  environment = 'Development';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('snooze-dev.myshopify') > -1 || window.location.href.indexOf('snooze-au.myshopify')) {
  environment = 'Staging';
} else if (window.location.href.indexOf('snooze.com.au') > -1) {
  environment = 'Production';
}

// internal search
var searchQuery = undefined;
if(window.location.href.indexOf('/search?q=') > -1){
  searchQuery = '';
}

var trafficType = undefined;


dataLayer.push({
    'company' : 'Snooze AU',
    'environment' : environment,
    'language' : 'en',
    'currency' : 'AUD',
    'customerType' : customerType,
    'pageType' : pageType,
    'breadcrumb' : breadcrumb,
    'originalPageURL' : window.location.href,
    'trafficType' : 'N/A',
    'clientId' : clientId,
    'adsBlocked' : 'N/A',
    'breakPoint' : breakpoint,
    'queryParams' : queryParams,
    'region' : 'N/A',
    'deviceChannel' : 'Responsive Website',

    
      'newsletterSubscriber' : undefined,
      'isLoggedIn' : undefined,
      'userGender' : 'N/A',
      'userPostalCode' : undefined,
      'userCity' : undefined,
      'userYearOfBirth' : 'N/A',
    

    

    

    

    

    // content group
    'contentGroup1' : contentGroup1,
    'contentGroup2' : contentGroupArray[0],
    'contentGroup3' : contentGroupArray[1],
    'contentGroup4' : 'N/A',
    'contentGroup5' : 'N/A',

});
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
  /*
    This function validates the e-mail address via a preg match of the "filter"
    @param1 string
    @return boolean
  */
  function validateEmail(isEmail) {
    "use strict";
      var filter = /^([\w-\.]+)@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.)|(([\w-]+\.)+))([a-zA-Z]{2,4}|[0-9]{1,3})(\]?)$/;

      if ( filter.test(isEmail) ) {
          return true;
      }
      else {
          return false;
      }
  }

  $('#contact_form').submit(function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var email = $(this).find('input[type=text]').val();

    window.identifyUser(email);

    if( email !== '' ) {
      if( validateEmail(email) ) {
        var counter = 0;
        $.ajax({
          url: 'https://api.system.snooze.com.au/ecom/subscribe',
          type: 'POST',
          cache: false,
          context: this,
          data: {
            email : email
          },
          success: function(data) {
            $(this).find('.input-group').hide();
            $(this).find('.custom-success').slideDown('fast');

            newsletterSignUpDataLayer({
              success: true,
              emailHash: md5(email),
            });
          },
          error: function(e) {
            $(this).find('.custom-error').find('.message').html("Subscription failed.");
            $(this).find('.custom-error').slideDown('fast');
            $(this).find('.btn').addClass('disabled').attr("disabled", true);

            newsletterSignUpDataLayer({
              success: false,
              error: e
            });
          }
        });
      } else {
        $(this).find('.custom-error').find('.message').html('Please enter a valid email');
        $(this).find('.custom-error').slideDown('fast');
        $(this).find('.btn').addClass('disabled').attr("disabled", true);
      }
    } else {
      $(this).find('.custom-error').find('.message').html('This field is required');
      $(this).find('.custom-error').slideDown('fast');
      $(this).find('.btn').addClass('disabled').attr("disabled", true);
    }
  });

  var typeCounter = 0;

  $('#Email, #mail').on('input', function() {
    if(typeCounter > 6){
      var email = $(this).val();

      if( email !== '' ) {
        if( validateEmail(email) ) {
          $(this).parents('.form-group').find('.custom-error').slideUp('fast');
          $(this).parents('.input-group').find('.btn').removeClass('disabled').removeAttr("disabled");
        } else {
          $(this).parents('.form-group').find('.custom-error').find('.message').html('Please enter a valid email');
          $(this).parents('.form-group').find('.custom-error').slideDown('fast');
          $(this).parents('.input-group').find('.btn').addClass('disabled').attr("disabled", true);
        }
      } else {
        $(this).parents('.form-group').find('.custom-error').find('.message').html('This field is required');
        $(this).parents('.form-group').find('.custom-error').slideDown('fast');
        $(this).parents('.input-group').find('.btn').addClass('disabled').attr("disabled", true);
      }
    }

    typeCounter++;
  });

</script>

    }

    typeCounter++;
  });

</script>
